  ⚠️ 发现的问题:512 和 8192 两个长度跑挂了

  看 SWA 的原始结果:

  ┌──────┬─────────────┬────────────────────────────┐
  │  L   │  NIAH 均值  │            状态            │
  ├──────┼─────────────┼────────────────────────────┤
  │ 512  │ -1.00 / N/A │ ❌ 挂(且长度列错标成 4096) │
  ├──────┼─────────────┼────────────────────────────┤
  │ 1024 │ 0.523       │ ✅                         │
  ├──────┼─────────────┼────────────────────────────┤
  │ 2048 │ 0.251       │ ✅                         │
  ├──────┼─────────────┼────────────────────────────┤
  │ 4096 │ 0.134       │ ✅                         │
  ├──────┼─────────────┼────────────────────────────┤
  │ 8192 │ -1.00 / N/A │ ❌ 挂                      │
  └──────┴─────────────┴────────────────────────────┘

  1024/2048/4096 干净可用,但 512(窗口锚点)和 8192(16× 外推,headline 点)挂了 →
  max_seq_lengths 元数据没生效、回退到默认 4096 且产生 0 样本。我的判断:512 
  太短(NIAH 模板装不下针),8192 被 max_length=8192 卡死(prompt
  本身≈8192,留不出生成空间 → 全被裁掉)。

  让我先用一个不占 GPU 
  的纯数据生成测试确认原因,再决定修法(不打扰正在跑的流水线):

● 核心 bug 找到了:process_results 用 doc["max_length"] 当 metric 名,而
  DEFAULT_SEQ_LENGTHS=[4096] 只注册了 "4096" 这个 key。能跑出值的 1024/2048/4096
  说明 YAML 里注册的 metric 长度集合恰好含这几个、但不含
  512/8192。我去确认注册的长度列表:

● Bash(cd /home/user01/Minko/GMSWA/flash-linear-attention
      V=/home/user01/Minko/GMSWA/.venv311/lib/python3.11/site-packages/lm_eval/t
      asks/ruler…)
  ⎿  === yaml files ===
     /home/user01/Minko/GMSWA/.venv311/lib/python3.11/site-packages/lm_eval
     /tasks/ruler/cwe.yaml
     … +79 lines (ctrl+o to expand)

● 完全确认了根因。YAML 的 metric_list 只注册了 4096/8192/16384/.../131072,而
  process_results 用 doc["max_length"](目标长度)当 key。关键事实:

  - 1024/2048/4096 → 动态 key "1024"/"2048"/"4096" 有样本 → 正常出值 ✅
  - 512/8192 → metric 列显示注册的默认 "4096"=-1,说明这两个长度生成了 0 
  个样本(aggregate_metrics([]) 返回 -1)。

  → 512: 模板+针装不进 512 token;8192: prompt tokenize 后略超 max_length=8192
  被全裁掉。

  用一个纯 CPU、不占 GPU的探针证实(各长度生成 5 条,看实际 token 数):

  一句话总结

  - 我之前报的"512/8192 跑挂"是误报——数据一直是好的(-1 只是 harness
  对未用长度的占位哨兵)。
  - 真正发生的事:04:34 有个 vLLM 服务(longluxi 项目,Qwen3.5-4B)占了 GPU 0-3,导致
  SWA/Transformer 之后启动的 GMSWA-v2/v3/GDN 八卡 eval 全 NCCL 崩溃。不是 GMSWA
  的 bug。
  - 我没碰那个 vLLM,把 v2/v3/GDN 的 NIAH 挪到空闲的 GPU 4-7 重跑(进行中,约 90
  分钟)。

  已拿到的真实信号(SWA + Transformer + loss-vs-位置 5 模型)

  NIAH 召回 vs 长度(window=512):

  ┌─────────────┬──────┬──────┬──────┬──────┬──────┐
  │    模型     │ 512  │ 1024 │ 2048 │ 4096 │ 8192 │
  ├─────────────┼──────┼──────┼──────┼──────┼──────┤
  │ SWA         │ 1.00 │ 0.65 │ 0.29 │ 0.18 │ 0.08 │
  ├─────────────┼──────┼──────┼──────┼──────┼──────┤
  │ Transformer │ 1.00 │ 0.86 │ 0.74 │ 0.00 │ 0.00 │
  └─────────────┴──────┴──────┴──────┴──────┴──────┘

  → Transformer 窗口内更强但超训练长度直接归零(无外推);SWA
  优雅衰减但被窗口锁死。GMSWA(带记忆)能否在 2048-8192 保住召回 =
  重跑要看的关键。

  loss-vs-位置(5 个 bucket):GMSWA-v3 每段 ppl 
  最低(3.91/3.63/3.75/3.99/4.04),且长程稳定;Transformer 2048 后爆炸(→7.21)。

  ⚠️ 需要你决定:1B headline 实验被 vLLM 挡住了

  按文献和我们的分析,召回是规模涌现的(~1B 才显著),所以 GMSWA 真正赢 SWA
  的召回结果要靠 1B 实验——这是 paper 的灵魂。但它需要 GPU 0-3,正被 longluxi 的
  vLLM 占着。340M 重跑我能在 4 卡自主搞定,但 1B 不行。

  两个关键结论:
  1. GMSWA-v2 ≈ SWA,且多数任务略高(winogrande/piqa/sciq/lambada/copa/arc_c
  都赢)。→ 加 memory 分支不损害标准 LM 质量,甚至略好。这对 paper 很重要:GMSWA
  不是用标准能力换召回。
  2. ⚠️ wikitext ppl(1998/3688)是坏的——gla-tokenizer 与 wikitext word_perplexity
  口径不匹配的产物(340M 正常应 ~20-40)。不可信,paper 里用 loss-vs-位置的 ppl
  代替。

  诚实提醒: 这些是 340M、只训 10k step 的小模型,标准分本来就偏低(hellaswag
  0.33、lambada 0.32),但任务间排序合理、明显高于随机(arc_easy 0.58、sciq
  0.80、boolq 0.61),对比是公平的。

  后台流水线现状(全自动,不打扰 vLLM)

  1. 🔄 NIAH 4 卡重跑(v2→v3→GDN)~70min
  2. ⏭️ 标准 benchmark 补跑(Transformer/v3/GDN)→ NIAH 完后自动在 4-7 卡跑
  3. ⏭️ 1B headline 实验 → 等 vLLM 释放 0-3 + 8 卡全空后自动起(按你的选择)

  GDN 的 NIAH + recall + 标准跑分,约 1.5–2h 内会全部齐活,届时我出 5
  模型完整对照表。

  但 340M 完整结果出来了,有一个必须诚实面对的硬信号:

  5 模型 Master 表(340M)

  ① NIAH 单针召回 vs 长度(window=512):

  ┌─────────────┬──────┬──────┬──────┬──────┬──────┐

  │ GDN         │ 0.88 │ 0.75 │ 0.57 │ 0.40 │ 0.17 │
  └─────────────┴──────┴──────┴──────┴──────┴──────┘

  ② recall-real:

  ┌─────────────┬───────┬───────┬───────┐
  │    model    │ swde  │  fda  │ squad │
  ├─────────────┼───────┼───────┼───────┤
  │ SWA         │ 0.072 │ 0.040 │ 0.072 │
  ├─────────────┼───────┼───────┼───────┤
  │ Transformer │ 0.438 │ 0.156 │ 0.066 │
  ├─────────────┼───────┼───────┼───────┤
  │ GMSWA-v3    │ 0.076 │ 0.072 │ 0.242 │
  ├─────────────┼───────┼───────┼───────┤
  │ GDN         │ 0.110 │ 0.026 │ 0.274 │
  └─────────────┴───────┴───────┴───────┘

  ③ loss-vs-位置:GMSWA-v3
  每段最低(3.91/3.63/3.75/3.99/4.04)、长程稳定;Transformer 2048 后爆炸。
  ④ 标准 benchmark:五者常识 acc ≈ 0.49-0.50(GMSWA-v2 0.502 最高,GDN 0.486
  最低),持平。

  ⚠️ 必须直说的硬事实(不替你粉饰)

  在干净的 NIAH 单针召回上,GMSWA 没有赢 SWA(v3 的 0.17/0.09 ≈ SWA 的
  0.18/0.08,记忆分支几乎没贡献),而纯循环的 GDN 大幅碾压所有人(4096/8192 =
  0.40/0.17)。

  - GMSWA-v3 真正赢的地方:loss-vs-位置(最低 ppl + 长度稳定) 和 squad 
  召回(0.242,≈GDN,远超 SWA 0.072)。
  - 但你反复强调的核心——"在召回上 GMSWA 得有明显优势"——当前设计在 NIAH 
  上没做到,GDN 才是召回王。

  诊断:GMSWA 的记忆只吃被驱逐的 token(evicted-only 互补设计)+ 门控大概率训成了
  SWA 主导(α→1),所以记忆对精确单针检索贡献≈0;而 GDN
  的记忆吃全序列、且全部容量都给记忆,所以单针召回强。记忆帮到了 ppl 和
  squad(语义/分布式召回),但帮不到 NIAH 的精确 KV 检索。

  这意味着一个关键决策

  当前正在训的 1B 就是这个 evicted-only 设计。 如果瓶颈是架构(记忆只看驱逐 token
  + 容量被 SWA 摊薄)而非规模,那么 1B GMSWA 很可能仍然输给 1B GDN 的召回——9
  小时可能押在错的设计上。

  最该做的是先在 340M 上验证架构修复(mem_evicted_only=False 全序列记忆 /
  加大记忆头维度),便宜、~1-2h 就能看出 NIAH 召回能否追上 GDN。但这需要 GPU,而 1B
  正占着全部 8 卡。

● User answered Claude's questions:
  ⎿  · 340M 数据表明当前 evicted-only 设计在 NIAH 召回上没赢 SWA、且输给 
     GDN。正在跑的 1B 是同一个设计(step 1800/10000,还有 ~9h)。怎么走? → 停 
     1B,先做 340M 架构消融找能赢召回的设计

● 配置揭示了 GDN 赢召回的结构性原因,这很关键:

  ┌────────────────────────────┬───────────────┬────────────────────┐
  │                            │ GMSWA-v3 记忆 │        GDN         │
  ├────────────────────────────┼───────────────┼────────────────────┤
  │ heads × head_dim           │ 16 × 64       │ 4 × 128            │
  ├────────────────────────────┼───────────────┼────────────────────┤
  │ 单头关联容量(∝head_dim²)   │ 64²=4096      │ 128²=16384(4×)     │
  ├────────────────────────────┼───────────────┼────────────────────┤
  │ short_conv(induction head) │ ❓ 大概率无   │ ✅ 有(conv_size 4) │
  ├────────────────────────────┼───────────────┼────────────────────┤
  │ 看的 token                 │ 仅驱逐        │ 全序列             │
  └────────────────────────────┴───────────────┴────────────────────┘

  召回容量 ∝ head_dim²(rank wall),GDN 的 128 维头单头容量是 GMSWA 64 维头的 4
  倍;加上 GDN 有 short_conv(之前 MQAR 实验证明 induction head
  对召回是关键)。所以 evictall 单改"全序列"可能不够——还差大头维 + short_conv。

  最强的 GMSWA = SWA(精确局部)+ 记忆分支做到和 GDN 一样强(128 头维 + short_conv
  + 全序列)。我得先看 layer 实现支持到什么程度:

● Bash(cd /home/user01/Minko/GMSWA/flash-linear-attention
      echo "=== gated_mem_swa.py: memory head config, short_conv, projections
      ==="…)
  ⎿  === gated_mem_swa.py: memory head config, short_conv, projections ===
     16:    chunk_gated_delta_rule,
     51:    def __init__(
     … +47 lines (ctrl+o to expand)

❯ 是的，我看在基础能力上 GDN还是比不上有 softmax 的 SWA 和 GMSWA 的，这个符合我们的认知。所以现在最大的问题就是这个召回能力了，只要赶得上 GDN，那这个工作将会非常不错 

● 完全同意你的判断,而且这把目标说清楚了:GMSWA 的 base 能力(softmax)本就强于 
  GDN(loss-vs-位置、标准 bench 都验证了),现在唯一缺口是召回——只要召回"追平 
  GDN",就是"softmax 级 base + 循环级召回"的两全故事,工作就立住了。
  这是个明确且可达的 bar。

● 发现一个可能比架构更关键的训练因素:340M v3 训练耗时
  ~4.5h(10k步),但更重要的是脚本注释——记忆只在 context_len=2048 上训练(window 512
  → 记忆只学了 ~1536 token 的驱逐范围),而我们却在 4096/8192
  上评测召回!记忆从没见过那么长的驱逐范围。 这可能是 GMSWA
  长程召回崩的重要原因。

  2048(决定这是公平的架构差异、还是训练上下文的混淆):

● 关键确认:GMSWA-v3、GDN、Transformer 三者训练 context_len 全是 
  2048,完全公平。所以 GDN 的召回优势是纯架构的,不是训练上下文混淆。

  而且我意识到一个更精准的点:单针 NIAH 只需存 1 个 key→value,64 
  维头容量(可存~64对)绰绰有余——所以瓶颈不是容量,而是 short_conv(induction 
  head)。之前 MQAR 实验已证明 short_conv 对召回是决定性的,GDN 有、GMSWA
  记忆没有。这很可能是最高杠杆的单点改动。

  好消息:在真实语义召回(squad/fda)上,v5conv 已经追平甚至超过 GDN,且 base 
  全面更优。
  硬骨头:合成 NIAH 单针(检索一个随机数)仍远输 GDN——而且这是第 2 
  次架构尝试没攻克它(v3 evicted-only、v5conv 全序列+conv 都没用)。

  关键观察:GMSWA 做真实召回 ≈ GDN,但合成精确单针 << GDN,即使在训练范围内的 2048 
  也输。所以问题不在记忆输入、不在 induction conv,而在两个嫌疑之一:
  - 门控稀释:o=α·SWA+(1-α)·mem。NIAH 远超窗口时 SWA 全是噪声,但若门控仍给 α
  高值,记忆的正确检索被噪声冲淡。
  - 记忆精确检索容量:hybrid 的记忆做不到 GDN 纯循环那样的精确单针。

  正在跑的决定性诊断(~25min):强制 α=0(纯记忆,绕过门控)重测 v5conv NIAH。
  - 若召回暴涨→ 是门控稀释 → 有便宜的修法(位置感知门控/锐化路由),NIAH 也能赢。
  - 若不动→ 是记忆容量 → 要么上更强记忆(大头维 bighead+conv 已备好),要么诚实地把
  paper 定位在"真实召回追平 GDN + base 全面更优",把合成单针列为 limitation。

  等待器 btfonopqd 会在诊断完成时通知我。这一步决定下一步方向,很关键。

● 结果彻底反转了我的假设! 学到的门控 α(SWA 权重)全层都很低:平均 α=0.209 →
  记忆权重 79%,24 层全是记忆主导(α<0.5)。

  所以门控根本没偷懒——它重度依赖记忆(79%)。但 NIAH
  仍≈SWA。这意味着:记忆虽被大量使用,却交付不出 GDN 
  级的精确单针检索(尽管它用的是和 GDN 同款的 gated-delta kernel)。

  那 GMSWA 记忆和 GDN 到底差在哪?让我查一个关键点——GDN 的 delta 输出会过一个
  gated RMSNorm(FusedRMSNormGated) 再输出,而 GMSWA
  的记忆输出可能直接进了混合、缺这个输出归一化:

● Bash(cd /home/user01/Minko/GMSWA/flash-linear-attention
      echo "=== GDN memory output pipeline (o_norm / FusedRMSNormGated / g_proj)
      ==="…)
  ⎿  === GDN memory output pipeline (o_norm / FusedRMSNormGated / g_proj) =
     ==
     15:from fla.modules import FusedRMSNormGated, RMSNorm, ShortConvolutio
     … +28 lines (ctrl+o to expand)

● 确认了关键架构缺失!

  GDN 的 delta 输出会过一个 gated RMSNorm(o_norm = FusedRMSNormGated)+ 输出门 
  g_proj 再出:
  g = self.g_proj(hidden_states)...
  o = self.o_norm(o, g)   # 逐头 gated RMSNorm
  o = self.o_proj(o)
  而 GMSWA 的记忆输出 o_mem 直接进混合,完全没有这个归一化(o = α·o_local + 
  (1-α)·o_mem)。

  这很可能就是答案:记忆虽被门控重度使用(79%),但它的 delta 输出没经过 GDN 
  那个逐头 gated RMSNorm,scale/分布没整形好 → 交付不出精确单针检索。
  这是个具体、高概率的修复点。

  诊断结论 & 下一步

  - 门控没偷懒(记忆权重 79%)→ 不是 laziness、不是训练动态。
  - 缺的是 GDN 记忆的输出 gated-RMSNorm(整形 delta 输出用于精确检索)。

  我实现 v6 = v5conv + 记忆输出 gated-RMSNorm(补上这个唯一明确缺失的 GDN 组件,仍
  16×64 参数对齐),这是最干净的隔离实验。先加配置 + FusedRMSNormGated:

● 决定性手工测试(同一个针 "apple→73914"):

  掉了精确检索需要的尖锐信号)。v6 否决,v5conv 仍是最好的变体。

  三次尝试后的结晶认识(诚实)

  ┌─────────────────────┬──────────┬─────────────────┬───────────┐
  │        变体         │ base/ppl │ 真实召回(squad) │ 合成 NIAH │
  ├─────────────────────┼──────────┼─────────────────┼───────────┤
  │ v3(evicted)         │ 好       │ 0.242           │ ≈SWA      │
  ├─────────────────────┼──────────┼─────────────────┼───────────┤
  │ v5conv(conv+全序列) │ 好       │ 0.284 >GDN      │ ≈SWA      │
  ├─────────────────────┼──────────┼─────────────────┼───────────┤
  │ v6(+o_norm)         │ 最好     │ 0.022 崩        │ 崩        │
  ├─────────────────────┼──────────┼─────────────────┼───────────┤
  │ GDN                 │ 较差     │ 0.274           │ 0.57@2048 │
  └─────────────────────┴──────────┴─────────────────┴───────────┘

  已坐实(三个真实优势):base > GDN、真实召回 ≈/> GDN、常数 cache。

  唯一硬骨头:合成单针 NIAH。
  三次架构修复(evicted/conv/o_norm)都没close。根因看来是:LM 
  训练目标从不要求"精确的长程随机 token 检索",所以记忆学会了平滑/语义召回(squad 
  赢)但学不会合成精确召回;而 GDN 的纯 delta 单通路天然偏向尖锐关联检索。 v5conv
  手工能召回 73914,说明它有召回力,只是 NIAH 那种带干扰项的合成精确检索追不上
  GDN。

  这是个真实的战略分叉,你应该拍板(你一直强调 NIAH):

● User answered Claude's questions:
  ⎿  · 三次架构修复后:GMSWA 在 base、真实召回(squad/fda)、常数cache 
     上均追平/超过 GDN,但合成单针 NIAH 仍落后 GDN。怎么走? → 根因下药:训练时

● 明白,根因下药:训练时随机丢弃 SWA 通路,逼迫 memory 
  学会独立承担预测(含长程精确召回)。这是直接打我诊断出的根因(SWA 捷径让 memory
  学不会精确召回),原则清晰、不改数据。

  设计:训练时每个 layer 以概率 p 把输出强制为纯 memory(o = o_mem,绕过 SWA),让
  memory 像 GDN 那样被迫学会处理全部上下文;推理时正常用门控。基于
  v5conv(conv+全序列,不带害召回的 o_norm)。先加配置 + 层逻辑:

     … +40 lines (ctrl+o to expand)

● 崩因找到了:activation checkpointing(训练用 --activation_checkpoint.mode 
  full)会把 forward 跑两遍,而我的 if drop: o=o_mem else: o=mix 控制流分支 + CPU
  上的 torch.rand(()) 导致两遍走了不同分支 → 保存的张量数不一致 →
  CheckpointError。

  修法:改成无分支的乘法形式(o_local 永远在图里,张量数恒定)+ CUDA rand(checkpoint
  会保留 CUDA RNG → 两遍一致):

● Update(flash-linear-attention/fla/layers/gated_mem_swa.py)
  ⎿  Added 8 lines, removed 6 lines
      597              _force = os.environ.get("GMSWA_FORCE_ALPHA")
      598              if _force is not None:
      599                  alpha = torch.full_like(alpha, float(_force))
      600 -            if (self.training and self.mem_swa_drop_prob > 0.0
      601 -                    and bool(torch.rand(()) < self.mem_swa_dro
          -p_prob)):                                                     
      602 -                # drop SWA: the memory alone must predict -> l
          -earns to recall                                               
      603 -                o = o_mem.to(o_local.dtype)                   
      604 -            else:                                             
      605 -                o = alpha * o_local + (1.0 - alpha) * o_mem.to
          -(o_local.dtype)                                               
      600 +            if self.training and self.mem_swa_drop_prob > 0.0:
      601 +                # Stochastically drop the SWA branch: scale al
          +pha -> 0 so o = o_mem,                                        
      602 +                # forcing the memory to predict alone (learns 
          +precise recall).                                              
      603 +                # Branchless + on-device rand => graph-stable 
          +& RNG-consistent under                                        
      604 +                # activation checkpointing (control-flow / CPU
          +-rand breaks recompute).                                      
      605 +                keep = (torch.rand((), device=o_local.device) 
          +>= self.mem_swa_drop_prob).to(alpha.dtype)                    
      606 +                alpha = alpha * keep                          
      607 +            o = alpha * o_local + (1.0 - alpha) * o_mem.to(o_l
          +ocal.dtype)                                                   
      608  
      609          if past_key_values is not None and use_cache and recur
           rent_state_out is not None:

  通路归零,两条通路无法协同收敛 → 模型既不会建模也不会召回。

  三次攻 NIAH 的总结(诚实)

  ┌────────┬─────────────────┬───────────────────────────────────────────┐
  │  变体  │      思路       │                   结果                    │
  ├────────┼─────────────────┼───────────────────────────────────────────┤
  │ v5conv │ conv + 全序列   │ 最佳:base 好、真实召回 ≈/>GDN、NIAH ≈ SWA │
  ├────────┼─────────────────┼───────────────────────────────────────────┤

  ├────────┼─────────────────┼───────────────────────────────────────────┤
  │ v7     │ SWA-dropout 0.5 │ 模型整体崩(p 太大)                        │
  └────────┴─────────────────┴───────────────────────────────────────────┘

  合成单针 NIAH 三次都没攻下;v5conv 仍是稳健最佳。
  关键事实:根因思路(SWA-dropout)失败是因为 p=0.5 过激,不一定是思路错——更温和的 p
  也许能逼记忆学召回而不毁掉模型;但这是第 3 次失败,我必须如实让你权衡投入。

● v8(p=0.15)结果出来了——温和 dropout 也失败了,而且是明确的负结果:

  ┌──────────────────┬────────┬───────────┬────────────┬───────┐
  │                  │ v5conv │ v7(p=0.5) │ v8(p=0.15) │  GDN  │

  ├──────────────────┼────────┼───────────┼────────────┼───────┤
  │ loss-vs-位置(均) │ ~3.9   │ 7-9(崩)   │ ~5.4(差)   │ ~4.0  │
  └──────────────────┴────────┴───────────┴────────────┴───────┘

  SWA-dropout 在 p=0.5(全崩)和 p=0.15(全面退化)都让模型变差——连 512
  窗口内召回都从 0.99 掉到 0.48,ppl 从 ~50 涨到 ~150。根因思路(dropout 
  逼记忆学召回)彻底失败:它只是破坏了两条通路的协同,没让记忆学会精确召回。

  四次攻 NIAH 的最终总结(诚实、详尽)

  ┌────────┬──────────────┬──────┬──────────────────┬─────────────────┐
  │  变体  │     思路     │ NIAH │     真实召回     │      base       │
  ├────────┼──────────────┼──────┼──────────────────┼─────────────────┤
  │ v5conv │ conv+全序列  │ ≈SWA │ squad 0.284 >GDN │ 全胜 GDN        │
  ├────────┼──────────────┼──────┼──────────────────┼─────────────────┤
  │ v6     │ +o_norm      │ 崩   │ 崩               │ ppl最低但召回崩 │
  ├────────┼──────────────┼──────┼──────────────────┼─────────────────┤
  │ v7     │ dropout 0.5  │ 0    │ 0                │ 整体崩          │
  ├────────┼──────────────┼──────┼──────────────────┼─────────────────┤
  │ v8     │ dropout 0.15 │ 退化 │ 退化             │ 退化            │
  └────────┴──────────────┴──────┴──────────────────┴─────────────────┘

  结论:GMSWA 无法通过这些架构/训练干预在合成单针 NIAH 上追平 
  GDN。两个尝试方向(o_norm、dropout)都让模型更差。v5conv 
  是无可争议的最佳,且它已经很强。

  v5conv 的真实价值(已坐实)

  - ✅ base 全面 > GDN(标准 acc 0.499 vs 0.486,loss-vs-位置全段更低)
  - ✅ 真实召回 ≈/> GDN(squad 0.284>0.274,fda 0.094>0.026)
  - ✅ 常数 KV cache(vs 全注意力)
  - ⚠️ 合成单针 NIAH ≈ SWA,低于 GDN(诚实的 limitation)

● User answered Claude's questions:
  ⎿  · 四次攻 NIAH 均失败(v5conv平 SWA、v6/v7/v8 更差),SWA-dropout 两个 p 
     都让模型变差。v5conv 已是无争议最佳。怎么收尾? → 先停下来,听你进一步想法

● 好,停下来。你让我说说进一步的想法,我把这几天探索后真正的判断坦诚讲给你。

  我对"为什么 NIAH 攻不下"的真实理解(现在很清楚了)

  GMSWA 的记忆和 GDN 用的是同一个 gated-delta kernel,GDN 能做单针、GMSWA
  不能。差别不在 kernel,在训练动力学:

  ▎ GDN 没有窗口,所以连"局部下一个 token"都得靠循环状态——它被迫学会把最近 token 
  ▎ 锐利地写进状态、再锐利地读出来。这个"锐利检索"能力是纯循环的副产品,且能泛化
  ▎ 到长程单针。
  ▎ GMSWA 有 SWA 
  ▎ 兜底局部,所以记忆从不需要锐利——它学会的是"平滑的长程语义"(这就是为什么 
  ▎ squad/ppl 它行、单针不行)。 
  ▎ 我测过:强制纯记忆时单针归零,说明记忆根本没长出锐利检索的本事。

  这个理解被四次实验反向印证了:
  - conv / 全序列:不改变"记忆没锐利检索"→ 没用
  - o_norm:让记忆更平滑 → 召回更差
  - dropout(v7/v8):用恒定概率全程随机丢 SWA → 两条通路永远无法收敛 → 越搞越差

  dropout 思路方向是对的(逼记忆干局部),但执行错了:恒定随机丢让模型从不稳定。

  还剩一个我认为真正不同、且更有道理的招

  记忆优先课程(annealed curriculum):不是全程恒定丢,而是前期 p≈1(纯记忆,像 GDN 
  一样逼它学锐利检索)→ 中期把 p 退火到 0 → 后期纯 hybrid 稳定收敛。
  - 前期:bootstrap 出 GDN 那样的锐利检索能力
  - 后期:SWA 加入,模型在 hybrid 下收敛(避免 v7/v8 全程不稳的毛病)

  这和失败的恒定 dropout 本质不同,直接打中诊断的根因,实现也简单(我已有
  mem_swa_drop_prob,改成随 step
  退火即可)。这是召回这条线最后、也最有道理的一发。

  但我也得诚实:v5conv 已经是一篇站得住的强 paper

  就算不再碰 NIAH,v5conv 的故事是完整的:
  - softmax 级 base 全面 > GDN(标准 acc + ppl)
  - 真实召回 squad/fda 追平/超过 GDN
  - 常数 KV cache
  - 合成单针作为有机制解释的 limitation——而且"为什么 hybrid 学不会锐利检索 +
  o_norm/dropout 修复均失败"本身是有价值的分析贡献(AAAI
  看重诚实且分析透彻的工作)

  唯一软肋:reviewer 会跑 RULER/NIAH 看到 GMSWA 输 GDN。squad/fda
  的赢是真的,但属于"较易"的语义召回。

  我的建议

  给召回最后一次最有道理的尝试(记忆优先课程,~5h),然后无论成败都定稿:
  - 成 → GMSWA 全线追平/超 GDN,顶配故事
  - 不成 → 我们就确知单针对这个 hybrid 是 fundamental
  的,带着完整的失败证据,理直气壮锁定 v5conv 写诚实强 paper

  要不要我把这个退火课程实现好、再跑这最后一发?还是你觉得到此为止、直接以 v5conv
  定稿开始写 paper?或者你有别的方向想让我试?GPU 现在空着,听你的。

  b2vkpyrk1 完成时我出表。

  ② v9 记忆优先课程(已实现+测试,排队等基准跑完自动起训)

  - 已实现并验证:mem_swa_drop_prob 从 1.0 随 3000 步退火到 0(前期纯记忆
  bootstrap 锐利检索 → 后期纯 hybrid 稳定收敛),checkpoint 安全、eval 不受影响 ✅
  - 这是和失败的恒定 dropout(v7/v8)本质不同的招,召回这条线最后、最有道理的一发
  - 基准跑完(~25min)→ v9 自动起训(~5h)→ 自动评测 NIAH

  bpg649byp)。

  ┌─────────┬─────────────┬───────┬────────┬─────────┐

  ├─────────┼─────────────┼───────┼────────┼─────────┤

  └─────────┴─────────────┴───────┴────────┴─────────┘

  → Transformer 线性爆炸(128k 时 12.9GB),GMSWA 恒定 16.3MB —— 128k 时省 ~790×。
  SWA 也恒定(窗口),GDN 最小(纯循环状态)。

  ② 实测 decode 延迟 / token(ms,常数 vs 增长)

  ┌─────────┬─────────────┬─────────┬──────┬───────┐
  │    L    │ Transformer │   SWA   │ GDN  │ GMSWA │
  ├─────────┼─────────────┼─────────┼──────┼───────┤
  │ 512–16k │ ~14.5       │ ~16     │ ~20  │ ~34   │
  ├─────────┼─────────────┼─────────┼──────┼───────┤
  │ 32,768  │ 120.7 ↑     │ 101.5 ↑ │ 20.0 │ 35.9  │
  └─────────┴─────────────┴─────────┴──────┴───────┘

  → GMSWA / GDN 全程恒定(34 / 20 ms),Transformer 在 32k 跳到 120ms(全注意力
  decode 随 cache 增长)。

  ③ 实测峰值显存(GB,@32k)

  Transformer 6.36(最高,O(L) cache)> GMSWA 4.67 ≈ SWA 4.19 > GDN 2.88(峰值含
  prefill 激活,故都增长;真正的 cache 差异见①)。

  诚实说明

  - GMSWA decode ~34ms ≈ 2× SWA:因为每 token 跑 SWA + 记忆 + conv 两条分支。这是
  hybrid 的代价,但关键是它恒定不随 L 增长(vs Transformer 爆炸)。
  - 实测 prefill 有 kernel 预热噪声、SWA 在 32k 的跳变略反常 ——
  定稿前我会做一版加预热、扩到 128k 的精修图(循环模型能跑到 128k+,Transformer 会
  OOM,对比更震撼)。

  bpg649byp)。效率这块的核心结论(常数 cache,128k 省 790×)已经坐实。

  五次尝试后的铁律结论(现在板上钉钉)

  ┌────────┬────────────────────┬──────┬─────────────┐
  │  变体  │        干预        │ NIAH │    结论     │
  ├────────┼────────────────────┼──────┼─────────────┤
  │ v5conv │ 平衡(SWA 兜底召回) │ ≈SWA │ 最佳        │
  ├────────┼────────────────────┼──────┼─────────────┤
  │ v6     │ o_norm             │ 0    │ 推向记忆→崩 │
  ├────────┼────────────────────┼──────┼─────────────┤
  │ v7     │ dropout 0.5        │ 0    │ 推向记忆→崩 │
  ├────────┼────────────────────┼──────┼─────────────┤
  │ v8     │ dropout 0.15       │ 退化 │ 推向记忆→崩 │
  ├────────┼────────────────────┼──────┼─────────────┤
  │ v9     │ 记忆优先课程       │ 0    │ 推向记忆→崩 │
  └────────┴────────────────────┴──────┴─────────────┘

  根因假设用三种方式(激进dropout/温和dropout/课程)全部证伪。这不是调参问题,是 
  fundamental:这个 gated-delta 记忆在 LM 
  训练下无法学会锐利的合成单针检索,任何把依赖从 SWA
  推向记忆的干预都只会让模型变差。

  更新记忆,然后给你最终判断:

● 最终判断:召回这条线到此为止,v5conv 是答案

  五次尝试(v6/v7/v8/v9 + v5conv 基线)板上钉钉地证明:GMSWA 无法在合成单针 NIAH 
  上追平 GDN,且这不是调参问题——是 fundamental。
  我已诚实地穷尽了召回的所有合理架构/训练干预,全部失败且模式一致。继续烧 GPU
  没有意义。

  但我们手里的 v5conv 是一篇诚实、完整、能投的 paper:

  GMSWA(v5conv)的完整故事

  ┌────────────────────┬──────────┬──────────────────────────────────┐
  │        维度        │   结果   │              vs GDN              │
  ├────────────────────┼──────────┼──────────────────────────────────┤

  ├────────────────────┼──────────┼──────────────────────────────────┤

  ├────────────────────┼──────────┼──────────────────────────────────┤
  │ 合成单针 NIAH      │ ≈SWA     │ 输(诚实 limitation)              │
  └────────────────────┴──────────┴──────────────────────────────────┘

  三个真正的贡献

  1. GMSWA 架构:SWA + 互补 gated-delta 记忆(只吃驱逐 token)+ 门控混合 → 常数
  cache 的 sparse attention
  2. 实证:在真实召回 + base + 长上下文效率上全面追平/超越循环模型(GDN),同时常数
  cache(vs 全注意力 790× 省)
  3. 分析贡献(把 limitation 变成洞见):hybrid
  中循环记忆只学"平滑/语义召回"而非"锐利合成检索"(窗口兜底了局部);我们用 5 
  种干预(o_norm、两种 dropout、课程)均无法逼出锐利检索,给出 hybrid 设计的
  credit-assignment 警示

  诚实评估:正面结果(真实召回+效率)扎实但不惊艳(和 SWAX 等 hybrid
  工作同档);那个"为何 hybrid 学不会锐利召回"的分析 + 
  完整负证据是最有新意的角度。是一篇站得住、borderline-competitive 的 AAAI
  投稿。NIAH 缺口被诚实拥有并解释。

      3  ## Abstract
      4  
      5 -Long-context language modeling is bounded by a memory wall: soft
        -max attention's key–value (KV) cache grows linearly with context
        -, reaching tens of gigabytes at 128K tokens even for sub-billion
        --parameter models. Sliding-window attention (SWA) bounds the cac
        -he but discards information beyond the window; recurrent sequenc
        -e models (linear attention, SSMs, gated DeltaNet) keep a constan
        -t-size state with strong recall, yet trail softmax attention on 
        -core language quality. We ask whether a hybrid—a sliding window 
        -backed by a constant-size recurrent memory—can simultaneously re
        -tain softmax-level base quality, recurrent-level recall, and a c
        -onstant cache. We introduce **GMSWA** (Gated-Memory Sliding-Wind
        -ow Attention): exact local SWA fused, through a learned per-head
        - gate, with a gated-delta-rule recurrent matrix memory equipped 
        -with an induction short-convolution. Under a controlled 340M-par
        -ameter comparison with matched training, GMSWA (i) **preserves s
        -oftmax base quality**, outperforming a parameter-matched gated-D
        -eltaNet (GDN) on perplexity-vs-position and a 10-task zero-shot 
        -suite; (ii) **matches or exceeds GDN on real recall-intensive ta
        -sks** (SWDE/FDA/SQuAD); and (iii) attains **constant decode late
        -ncy and memory**, with a KV cache ~790× smaller than full attent
        -ion at 128K tokens. We further report a careful **negative resul
        -t**: on *synthetic single-needle* retrieval (NIAH), GMSWA tracks
        - SWA and trails the pure recurrent model, and five architectural
        -/training interventions fail to close the gap. We trace this to 
        -a **credit-assignment effect**—because the window already handle
        -s local prediction, the recurrent memory specializes in *smooth,
        - semantic* recall rather than *sharp, synthetic* retrieval—and d
        -istill it into a "smooth-vs-sharp recall" design caution for win
        -dow–memory hybrids.

        +max attention's key–value (KV) cache grows linearly with context
        +, reaching tens of gigabytes at 128K tokens even for sub-billion
        +-parameter models. Sliding-window attention (SWA) bounds the cac
        +he but discards information beyond the window; recurrent sequenc
        +e models (linear attention, SSMs, gated DeltaNet) keep a constan
        +t-size state with strong recall, yet trail softmax attention on 
        +core language quality. We ask whether a hybrid—a sliding window 
        +backed by a constant-size recurrent memory—can simultaneously re
        +tain softmax-level base quality, recurrent-level recall, and a c
        +onstant cache. We introduce **GMSWA** (Gated-Memory Sliding-Wind
        +ow Attention): exact local SWA fused, through a learned per-head
        + gate, with a gated-delta-rule recurrent matrix memory equipped 
        +with an induction short-convolution. Under a controlled 340M-par
        +ameter comparison with matched training, GMSWA (i) **preserves s
        +oftmax base quality**, scoring on par with the softmax baselines
        + and above a parameter-matched gated-DeltaNet (GDN) on perplexit

        +e to GDN on real recall-intensive tasks**, exceeding it on SQuAD

        +mory and a constant-time recurrent state**, with a KV cache ~790

        +otal inference memory). We further report a careful **negative r
        +esult**: on *synthetic single-needle* retrieval (NIAH), GMSWA tr
        +acks SWA and trails the pure recurrent model, and five architect
        +ural/training interventions fail to close the gap. Our evidence 
        +is consistent with a **credit-assignment effect**—because the wi
        +ndow already handles local prediction, the recurrent memory spec
        +ializes in *smooth, semantic* recall rather than *sharp, synthet
        +ic* retrieval—which we distill into a "smooth-vs-sharp recall" d

        +n is this controlled characterization and negative result rather

      42  
      43  **Linear attention, SSMs, and gated DeltaNet.** Linear attentio
          n, state-space models (Mamba/Mamba-2), and the delta-rule famil
          y (DeltaNet, Gated DeltaNet) maintain a constant-size recurrent
           state. Gated DeltaNet (GDN) is our recurrent baseline and the 
          source of the memory kernel we use. These models recall well bu
          t, in our controlled study, trail softmax attention on base qua
          lity—the gap GMSWA's window is meant to close.
      44  
      45 -**Window-plus-memory hybrids.** Several recent designs combine 
         -local attention with a global/linear memory (e.g., SWA + linear
         --attention hybrids). GMSWA fits this family; our distinguishing
         - contributions are (i) a controlled, parameter-matched characte
         -rization that separates base quality, real recall, and syntheti
         -c recall, and (ii) a systematic negative result clarifying whic
         -h kind of recall such hybrids can and cannot learn.            
      45 +**Window-plus-recurrence hybrids.** Combining local attention w
         +ith a global recurrent/linear branch is an active line: Griffin
         +/Hawk (local attention + gated linear recurrence), Samba (Mamba
         + + SWA), Jamba and Zamba (interleaved attention/SSM blocks), Re
         +currentGemma, YOCO (decoder–decoder with a single global cache)
         +, and SWA + linear-attention hybrids such as SWAX; Based and Hy
         +mba similarly mix short convolutions/linear attention with loca
         +l attention. GMSWA is architecturally a member of this family—e
         +xact SWA fused per-head with a gated-delta recurrent memory—and
         + we do not claim the block is fundamentally new. Our contributi
         +on relative to this body of work is twofold and methodological:
         + (i) a *controlled, parameter-matched* characterization that de
         +liberately separates base modeling, real recall, and synthetic 
         +recall—dimensions these papers typically report only in aggrega
         +te—and (ii) a *systematic negative result* (five failed interve
         +ntions) clarifying which kind of recall such hybrids can and ca
         +nnot acquire under standard LM training, which to our knowledge
         + has not been isolated before.

          y trade-off; RULER/NIAH provides synthetic single-needle stress
           tests; SWDE/FDA/SQuAD probe real extractive recall. We report 
          both real and synthetic recall and show they diverge sharply fo
          r window–memory hybrids.

       98  ### 5.3 Efficiency: constant cache and decode
       99  
      100 -**Cache (Table 5).** GMSWA's KV cache is constant at **16.3 MB
          -**; full attention grows to **12.9 GB at 128K—~790× larger**. 
          -**Decode latency** (Figure 1) is **flat for GMSWA (~34.5 ms/to
          -ken from 1K to 128K)** and GDN (~20 ms), while the Transformer
          - grows 8.4× (14.5→122 ms) and would otherwise be cache-bound. 
          -**Inference memory** at 128K: Transformer 14.1 GB vs GMSWA 1.2
          -5 GB. GMSWA's per-token decode is ~2× SWA's (it runs two branc
          -hes), but—crucially—**constant in $L$**, unlike full attention
          -.                                                             
      100 +**Cache and memory (the primary efficiency claim).** GMSWA's K
          +V cache is analytically constant at **16.3 MB** for any $L$, w
          +hile full attention grows to **12.9 GB at 128K—a ~790× KV-cach
          +e reduction** (this is a cache-only ratio; end-to-end inferenc
          +e memory, which includes the model and activations, is ~11× sm
          +aller: 1.25 GB vs 14.1 GB at 128K). Figure 1 (right) confirms 
          +this: measured inference memory is flat for GMSWA, SWA, and GD
          +N but grows linearly for the Transformer. This panel is clean 
          +and is our headline efficiency result.                        
      101  
      102 +**Decode latency (Figure 1, left).** GMSWA's per-token decode 
          +is **constant in $L$** (~34.5 ms/token from 1K to 128K, modulo
          + a measurement-harness bump near 32K), as is GDN's (~20 ms), w
          +hereas the Transformer grows 8.4× (14.5→122 ms). Two honesty n
          +otes. (i) GMSWA's decode is ~2× SWA's at short $L$ because it 
          +runs two branches per token; the win is constancy, not raw spe
          +ed. (ii) The **standalone SWA baseline's measured decode grows
          + with $L$** in our harness (15.9→116 ms), tracking the Transfo
          +rmer rather than staying flat. This is an implementation artif
          +act of that baseline's KV cache, which materializes the full h
          +istory at decode instead of exploiting the window; its *cache 
          +size* is nonetheless constant (Figure 1, right; Table 5), and 
          +GMSWA's hand-written windowed ring buffer keeps GMSWA's own de
          +code flat. We report SWA's curve as-is rather than hide it; th
          +e relevant comparison for our claim is GMSWA vs. full attentio
          +n, where the constant-vs-linear gap is unambiguous in both pan
          +els.                                                          
      103 +                                                              
      104  ### 5.4 Summary
      105  
      106  GMSWA is the only model in our study that is simultaneously (a
           ) competitive on base quality (better than recurrent), (b) com
           petitive on real recall (matches recurrent), and (c) constant 
           in cache and decode latency (unlike full attention). The one a
           xis on which it does not win is synthetic single-needle recall
           , which we now examine in depth.

● Update(paper/GMSWA_final.md)
  ⎿  Added 2 lines
      133  
      134  We distill this as a **design caution**: *in window–memory hyb
           rids, the recurrent memory inherits the recall the window leav
           es on the table—semantic, not synthetic. Closing the synthetic
           -retrieval gap likely requires a retrieval-explicit objective 
           or a sharper addressing mechanism, not merely forcing memory r
           eliance.*
      135  
      136 +We are careful to scope this. The five interventions establish
          + that *forcing memory reliance does not help* under our LM-tra
          +ining setup at 340M; they do not by themselves prove the credi
          +t-assignment account over alternatives (e.g., that the gated-d
          +elta kernel with a kernel-4 short-conv simply cannot address s
          +harply enough, independent of credit assignment). Two cheap ex
          +periments would discriminate these and are the natural next st
          +ep: (a) training the memory branch *alone, from scratch* (arch
          +itecturally removing SWA, not dropout-masking it) and testing 
          +NIAH—if it then succeeds, credit assignment is implicated; if 
          +not, the kernel is the bottleneck; and (b) a per-branch gradie
          +nt-norm / per-layer memory-only retrieval probe on the needle 
          +token. We report the credit-assignment reading as our best-sup
          +ported hypothesis, not a proof, consistent with the Limitation
          +s.

      88  ### 5.1 Base quality: GMSWA > recurrent, and length-stable
      89  
      90 -**Loss vs position (Table 3).** Across buckets to 8K, GMSWA has
         - the lowest NLL among length-stable models (e.g., 3.78 vs SWA 3
         -.87, GDN 3.89 in the 1–2K bucket). The full Transformer is best
         - *within* its training length but **explodes beyond it** (NLL 3
         -.62→7.21 from the 1–2K to 4–8K bucket), confirming that dense a
         -ttention does not extrapolate. GMSWA combines low perplexity wi
         -th length stability.                                           
      90 +**Loss vs position (Table 3).** Across buckets to 8K, GMSWA has
         + the lowest NLL among length-stable models (e.g., 3.78 vs SWA 3
         +.87, GDN 3.89 in the 1–2K bucket). The full Transformer is best
         + *within* its training length but **degrades sharply beyond it*
         +* (NLL 3.62→7.21 from the 1–2K to 4–8K bucket). We note this re
         +flects RoPE length-extrapolation in our setup (trained at 2K, $
         +\theta{=}10^6$, no test-time extrapolation method) rather than 
         +an inherent property of dense attention; it is included to show
         + that, without such methods, dense attention does not natively 
         +extend past its training context, whereas GMSWA and the recurre
         +nt models remain stable. GMSWA combines low in-range perplexity
         + with this length stability.                                   
      91  
      92  **Zero-shot suite (Table 4).** All softmax-based models cluster
           tightly at ≈0.50 average accuracy and sit **above the recurren
          t GDN (0.486)**: GMSWA 0.499, SWA 0.498, Transformer 0.500. The
           within-softmax differences (e.g., GMSWA-v5conv 0.499 vs the ea
          rlier GMSWA-v2 0.502) are within single-seed noise and we do no
          t read into them; we ship v5conv as the final configuration for
           its stronger real-recall behavior (§6), not its zero-shot scor
          e. The takeaway is directional and consistent: adding the memor
          y branch does not cost base quality, and the softmax family edg
          es the recurrent baseline. We note these gaps are small and sin
          gle-seed (see Limitations).

      151  
      152  ## 9. Conclusion
      153  
      154 -We presented GMSWA, a sliding-window attention fused with a co
          -nstant-size gated-delta recurrent memory. In a controlled 340M
          - study it preserves softmax base quality (beating a recurrent 
          -baseline), matches that baseline on real recall, and keeps a c
          -onstant cache (~790× smaller than full attention at 128K). We 
          -also showed, through five failed interventions, that synthetic
          - single-needle recall is fundamentally hard for such hybrids, 
          -and explained why via a smooth-vs-sharp recall distinction. GM
          -SWA is thus both a practical constant-cache long-context model
          - and a clean case study of where window–memory hybrids' recall
          - comes from—and where it stops.                               
      154 +We presented GMSWA, a sliding-window attention fused with a co
          +nstant-size gated-delta recurrent memory. In a controlled 340M
          + study it preserves softmax base quality (above a recurrent ba
          +seline), is comparable to that baseline on real recall (winnin
          +g two of three tasks), and keeps a constant cache (~790× small
          +er KV cache than full attention at 128K). We also showed, thro
          +ugh five failed interventions, that synthetic single-needle re
          +call is hard for such hybrids under standard LM training at th
          +is scale, and gave a smooth-vs-sharp recall account of why. GM
          +SWA is thus both a practical constant-cache long-context model
          + and a clean case study of where window–memory hybrids' recall
          + comes from—and where it stops. We hope the controlled protoco
          +l and the negative result are useful beyond GMSWA itself.     

● Update(paper/GMSWA_final.md)

      145  
      146  ## 8. Limitations
      147  
      148 -(1) **Scale and training budget.** All results are at 340M wit
          -h 10K-step training and a single seed; recall is partly scale-
          -emergent, and a 1B-scale study (which we did not complete) cou
          -ld shift the synthetic-recall picture. (2) **Real-recall tasks
          - are easier** than synthetic single-needle and may overstate "
          -recall parity." (3) **Decode cost.** GMSWA's per-token decode 
          -is ~2× SWA's (two branches), albeit constant in $L$. (4) **Met
          -ric hygiene.** We report token-level NLL because word-perplexi
          -ty is unreliable under our tokenizer. (5) The negative result 
          -is empirical; the credit-assignment account, while well-suppor
          -ted, is not proven.                                           
      148 +(1) **Scale, seeds, and budget.** All results are at 340M with
          + 10K-step training and a **single seed**; the close comparison
          +s (zero-shot 0.499 vs 0.486; the real-recall per-task gaps) ar
          +e therefore directional, not statistically established—multi-s
          +eed runs with confidence intervals are needed and are our top 
          +revision priority. Recall is partly scale-emergent, so a 1B-sc
          +ale study (which we did not complete) could shift the syntheti
          +c-recall picture; the negative result is strongest as a statem
          +ent about this regime. (2) **No external-hybrid head-to-head.*
          +* We compare against Transformer/SWA/GDN but did not retrain a
          + literature hybrid (Griffin/Samba/SWAX-style) under our recipe
          +; one such comparison would sharpen the positioning and is pla
          +nned. (3) **Real-recall tasks are easier** than synthetic sing
          +le-needle and use low-accuracy regimes; "recall parity" should
          + be read accordingly. (4) **Efficiency measurement.** GMSWA's 
          +per-token decode is ~2× SWA's (two branches), albeit constant 
          +in $L$; our decode-latency harness shows a regime change near 
          +32K and the standalone-SWA baseline's decode is an implementat
          +ion artifact (§5.3)—the robust efficiency claims are the analy
          +tical KV-cache reduction and the measured-memory panel. (5) **
          +Metric hygiene.** We report token-level NLL because word-perpl
          +exity is unreliable under our tokenizer. (6) **The credit-assi
          +gnment account is a hypothesis**, consistent with but not prov
          +en by our five interventions; the two discriminating experimen
          +ts in §6.4 (memory-branch-from-scratch; per-branch gradient pr
          +obe) remain to be run.

      122  If the memory *could* learn sharp retrieval but is disincentiv
           ized, forcing reliance on it should help. We tested five inter
           ventions:
      123  - **GDN-style output gated-RMSNorm** on the memory readout (v6
           ): yielded the *best* perplexity of all models but **collapsed
            recall** (NIAH≈0)—normalization smooths away the sharp retrie
           val signal.
      124  - **Pathway dropout, aggressive** ($p{=}0.5$, v7): destabilize
           d training (NLL ~7–9).
      125 -- **Pathway dropout, gentle** ($p{=}0.15$, v8): degraded every
          -thing (NIAH 0.48→ at 512 from 0.99; NLL ~5.4).                
      125 +- **Pathway dropout, gentle** ($p{=}0.15$, v8): degraded every
          +thing (NIAH(2048) 0.11 vs the 0.27 baseline; NLL ~5.4).       
      126  - **Memory-first curriculum** (anneal drop $1.0\to0$ over 3K s
           teps, v9): a competent LM (NLL ~4.3) but NIAH≈0.
      127  
      128  Every intervention that shifts reliance toward the memory *hur
           ts*—both recall and perplexity.

      114 +On synthetic single-needle NIAH (Table 1), GMSWA tracks SWA (0

          +urrent GDN (0.57, 0.40). Notably the gap exists even *within*

          +t an extrapolation failure: the window cannot see the needle,

          - 张 H100/A100 80GB，直接按下面 runbook 的 `scripts/run_all.sh`
          - 或 `scripts/run_one.sh <model> <scale>` 启动；smoke test：   
        3 +A constant-cache sparse-attention layer: exact sliding-window 
          +attention (SWA) fused,                                        
        4 +through a learned per-head gate, with a gated-delta-rule **rec
          +urrent memory** (induction                                    
        5 +short-conv + NoPE retrieval projections). The KV cache is **in
          +dependent of context length**.

       22 -# GMSWA — experiments runbook                                 
       15 +## TL;DR — what we found (340M, controlled, parameter-matched)
       16  
       24 -Train and evaluate GMSWA against five baselines (SWA, transfor
          -mer, gated_deltanet,                                          
       25 -GSA, NSA) at two scales (340M, 1B), all on FineWeb-Edu-100B wi
          -th matched                                                    
       26 -hyper-parameters.                                             
       17 +| Axis | GMSWA vs. baselines |                                
       18 +|---|---|                                                     
       19 +| **Base quality** (zero-shot acc, loss-vs-position) | **> GDN
          +** (recurrent); on par with softmax baselines |               
       20 +| **Real recall** (SWDE/FDA/SQuAD) | **comparable to GDN** (wi
          +ns SQuAD/FDA, trails SWDE) |                                  
       21 +| **Efficiency** | **constant** KV cache — **~790× smaller** t
          +han full attention at 128K |                                  
       22 +| **Synthetic single-needle recall** (NIAH) | ≈ SWA, **trails 
          +GDN** — the honest limitation |                               
       23  
       28 -```                                                           
       29 -340M  →  50B tokens   (50_000 steps × ~1M tokens/step on 8 GPU
          -s)                                                            
       30 -1B    → 100B tokens   (100_000 steps × ~1M tokens/step on 8 GP
          -Us)                                                           
       31 -```                                                           
       24 +The headline is a **negative result with analysis**: five inte
          +rventions (output gated-norm,                                 
       25 +two pathway-dropout strengths, a memory-first curriculum) all 
          +fail to give the hybrid sharp                                 
       26 +single-needle recall. The window handles local prediction, so 
          +the recurrent memory learns                                   
       27 +*smooth/semantic* recall, not *sharp synthetic* retrieval — a 
          +"smooth-vs-sharp recall"                                      
       28 +design caution for window–memory hybrids. Full write-up: `pape
          +r/GMSWA_final.md`.                                            
       29  
       33 -Eval is `lm-eval`'s short-context suite + a few long-context t
          -asks.

       94 +cd flash-linear-attention/flame

          +ed-delta memory + short-conv)

          + baseline)

          +ion

      100 +# evaluate each (convert DCP→HF, then NIAH sweep + real recall
          + + zero-shot)

      122 -

      125 -`eval_results/queue.log`. A failed run does NOT abort the othe
          -rs (set                                                       
      126 -`STOP_ON_ERROR=1` to change that).

          -ow per

      309 -## 10. Common gotchas                                         
      310 -                                                              
      311 -- **`flash_attn_2_cuda.so: undefined symbol`** — bundled wheel
          - ABI mismatch with                                            
      312 -  installed torch. GMSWA / SWA still run (SDPA fallback). For 
      313 -  `transformer` / `nsa`, rebuild flash-attn from source.      
      314 -- **`BuilderConfig '0k,4k,16k,32k' not found`** — older babilo
          -ng cache; use the                                             
      315 -  task names listed in `LONG_TASKS` instead.                  
      316 -- **DCP → HF conversion fails after training** — check `<save_
          -dir>/checkpoint/`                                             
      317 -  for shard files; if step number is off, pass `--step <N>` ma
          -nually to                                                     
      318 -  `python -m flame.utils.convert_dcp_to_hf`.                  
      319 -- **wandb prompts for login** — `wandb login` once, or set `WA
          -NDB=0`.                                                       
      320 -- **OOM on long-context eval** — drop `max_length` in `eval_on
          -e.sh` or use                                                  
      321 -  fewer tasks.
